Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32 and BizViz

🌐 Source: Cisa • 📅 27 Oct 2026 • ⏱️ 473 min read (94688 words, 📚 Deep Dive)
💡 Key Takeaways
  • It has been revealed that view CSAF Summary Successful exploitation of these vulnerabilities could allow an attacker to execute arbitrary code (CVE-2023-4863), bypass authentication (CVE-2026-16436 and CVE-2026-16437), or perform arbitrary operations (CVE-2026-16438), resulting in information disclosure, tampering with, destruction, or deletion of information, and a denial-of-service (DoS) condition of the system.
  • Successful exploitation of these vulnerabilities could allow an attacker to execute arbitrary code (CVE-2023-4863), bypass authentication (CVE-2026-16436 and CVE-2026-16437), or perform arbitrary operations (CVE-2026-16438), resulting in information disclosure, tampering with, destruction, or deletion of information, and a denial-of-service (DoS) condition of the system.
  • The following versions of Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32, BizViz, Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, GENESIS32 and BizViz are affected:.
📑 Quick Jump: 1. Overview • 2. Key Details • 3. Closing Notes

It has been revealed that view CSAF Summary Successful exploitation of these vulnerabilities could allow an attacker to execute arbitrary code (CVE-2023-4863), bypass authentication (CVE-2026-16436 and CVE-2026-16437), or perform arbitrary operations (CVE-2026-16438), resulting in information disclosure, tampering with, destruction, or deletion of information, and a denial-of-service (DoS) condition of the system. The following versions of Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32, BizViz, Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, GENESIS32 and BizViz are affected: Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32, BizViz, Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, GENESIS32 and BizViz 3 Vulnerabilities Out-of-bounds Write, Weak Password Recovery Mechanism for Forgotten Password, Incorrect Privilege Assignment Background Critical Infrastructure Sectors: Critical Manufacturing Countries/Areas Deployed: Worldwide Company Headquarters Location: Japan, United States Vulnerabilities Expand All + CVE-2023-4863 Out-of-bounds Write (CWE-787) vulnerability caused by libwebp, an internal component in GENESIS64 and ICONICS Suite could allow an authenticated local attacker to cause a heap-based buffer overflow and execute arbitrary code by importing a crafted file as an administrator for the affected product. As a result, the attacker may disclose internal information, tamper with, destroy, or delete internal information, or cause the affected system to enter a denial-of-service (DoS) condition. Read More 4 Affected Products Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS64: >=10.97|<=10.97.2 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.97.3 or later for GENESIS64 and ICONICS Suite. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64 is included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf". MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. Additional Metrics Relevant CWE: CWE-787 Out-of-bounds Write CVSS Version Base Score Base Severity Vector String 3.1 8.2 HIGH CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:H/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H 4.0 9.3 CRITICAL CVSS:4.0/AV:L/AC:L/AT:N/PR:H/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:H/SI:H/SA:H View CVE Details CVE-2026-16436 Weak Password Recovery Mechanism for Forgotten Password (CWE-640) vulnerability in GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32, and BizViz could allow a remote attacker to unlock the system by obtaining the challenge response by some means. The challenge response used to unlock the system is common to all users and fixed. As a result, the attacker may disclose, tamper with, destroy, or delete internal information, or cause the affected system to enter a denial-of-service (DoS) condition. For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf". MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account. Mitsubishi Electric MobileHMI: <=10.97.3 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf". MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions whitepaper on security vulnerabilities, which can be found at "https://iconics.com/about/security/cert". MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account. Additional Metrics Relevant CWE: CWE-640 Weak Password Recovery Mechanism for Forgotten Password CVSS Version Base Score Base Severity Vector String 3.1 8.1 HIGH CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:H/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H 4.0 9.2 CRITICAL CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:P/PR:N/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N View CVE Details CVE-2026-16437 Weak Password Recovery Mechanism for Forgotten Password (CWE-640) vulnerability in GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32, and BizViz could allow an authenticated local user with administrative privileges to unlock the system by tampering with the management file that stores the challenge response. Administrators are able to read and write the management file. But the management file is protected by Windows. As a result, the attacker may disclose, tamper with, destroy, or delete internal information, or cause the affected system to enter a denial-of-service (DoS) condition. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account. Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS32: vers:all/* Product Status: known_affected Remediations No fix plannedMitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions has no plans to release fixed versions for GENESIS32 or BizViz. For customers using GENESIS32 or BizViz, Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommends taking the mitigations described in the Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions' whitepaper which can be found at "https://iconics.com/about/security/cert". Alternatively, customers should consider replacing GENESIS32 or BizViz with GENESIS64 or GENESIS. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. Additional Metrics Relevant CWE: CWE-640 Weak Password Recovery Mechanism for Forgotten Password CVSS Version Base Score Base Severity Vector String 3.1 6.4 MEDIUM CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:H/PR:H/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H 4.0 7.1 HIGH CVSS:4.0/AV:L/AC:H/AT:N/PR:H/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:N/SI:N/SA:N View CVE Details CVE-2026-16438 Incorrect Privilege Assignment (CWE-266) vulnerability in GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, and MC Works64 could allow an attacker who can log in using the same administrator account for the affected products to launch and operate DCOM objects locally or remotely. During installation, the installers of the affected products unnecessarily grant default DCOM permissions to the administrator account of the affected products. As a result, the attacker may disclose, tamper with, destroy, or delete internal information, or cause the affected system to enter a denial-of-service (DoS) condition. Read More 53 Affected Products Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS64: >=10.97|<=10.97.2 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Mitsubishi Electric ICONICS Suite: >=10.97|<=10.97.2 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS64: >=10.97|<=10.97.2 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions ICONICS Suite: >=10.97|<=10.97.2 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS: <=11.03 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS64: <=10.97.3 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf". MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions Hyper Historian: <=10.97.3 Product Status: known_affected Remediations Vendor fixMitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions whitepaper on security vulnerabilities, which can be found at "https://iconics.com/about/security/cert". MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. Additional Metrics Relevant CWE: CWE-266 Incorrect Privilege Assignment CVSS Version Base Score Base Severity Vector String 3.1 9.1 CRITICAL CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:H/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H 4.0 9.4 CRITICAL CVSS:4.0/AV:N/AC:L/AT:N/PR:H/UI:N/VC:H/VI:H/VA:H/SC:H/SI:H/SA:H View CVE Details Acknowledgments Mitsubishi Electric discovered these vulnerabilities. Recommended Practices CISA recommends users take defensive measures to minimize the risk of exploitation of these vulnerabilities. Minimize network exposure for all control system devices and/or systems, ensuring they are not accessible from the internet. Locate control system networks and remote devices behind firewalls and isolating them from business networks. When remote access is required, use more secure methods, such as Virtual Private Networks (VPNs), recognizing VPNs may have vulnerabilities and should be updated to the most current version available. Also recognize VPN is only as secure as the connected devices. CISA reminds organizations to perform proper impact analysis and risk assessment prior to deploying defensive measures. CISA also provides a section for control systems security recommended practices on the ICS webpage on cisa.gov/ics. Several CISA products detailing cyber defense best practices are available for reading and download, including Improving Industrial Control Systems Cybersecurity with Defense-in-Depth Strategies. CISA encourages organizations to implement recommended cybersecurity strategies for proactive defense of ICS assets. Additional mitigation guidance and recommended practices are publicly available on the ICS webpage at cisa.gov/ics in the technical information paper, ICS-TIP-12-146-01B--Targeted Cyber Intrusion Detection and Mitigation Strategies. Organizations observing suspected malicious activity should follow established internal procedures and report findings to CISA for tracking and correlation against other incidents. This is republished to CISA's website as a means of increasing visibility and is provided "as-is" for informational purposes only. CISA is not responsible for the editorial or technical accuracy of republished advisories and provides no warranties of any kind regarding any information contained within this advisory. Successful exploitation of these vulnerabilities could allow an attacker to execute arbitrary code (CVE-2023-4863), bypass authentication (CVE-2026-16436 and CVE-2026-16437), or perform arbitrary operations (CVE-2026-16438), resulting in information disclosure, tampering with, destruction, or deletion of information, and a denial-of-service (DoS) condition of the system.

The following versions of Mitsubishi Electric GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, MC Works64, GENESIS32, BizViz, Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, HyperHistorian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI, IoTWorX, GENESIS32 and BizViz are affected:.

Product Status: known_affected.

Vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.97.3 or later for GENESIS64 and ICONICS Suite. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64 is included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf".

MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately patch it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Industry observers note that mitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately enhancement it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers using the affected product who cannot immediately update it, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

In a recent development, mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account.

(1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account.

Product Status: known_affected.

Recent findings suggest that vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf".

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

In a recent development, mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account.

According to recent reports, vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf".

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Sources indicate that mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account.

Product Status: known_affected.

It has been revealed that vendor fixMitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for IoTWorX. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://hub.docker.com/r/iconics/iotworx". For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions whitepaper on security vulnerabilities, which can be found at "https://iconics.com/about/security/cert".

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator users, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Recent findings suggest that mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend performing the following steps for GENESIS, GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, MobileHMI and MC Works64, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability. (1) Delete the .dat file at the location specified below. This file name and location is different depending on the product version. (Version 10: C:\Program Files\ICONICS\GENESIS64\Components\ClientChallengeTable.dat; Version 11: C:\ProgramData\ICONICS\11\SecurityChallenges.dat) (2) Create a blank file with the same name and location. (3) Restrict the file system write and delete access to this file to a unique administrator account, different than the GENESIS administrator account.

Product Status: known_affected.

No fix plannedMitsubishi Electric has no looks to release fixed versions for GENESIS32 or BizViz. For customers using GENESIS32 or BizViz, Mitsubishi Electric recommends taking the mitigations described in the Mitsubishi Electric's security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf". Alternatively, customers should consider replacing GENESIS32 or BizViz with GENESIS64 or GENESIS.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator owners, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately patch the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Relevant CWE: CWE-640 Weak Password Recovery Mechanism for Forgotten Password.

Sources indicate that vendor fixMitsubishi Electric is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric security advisory at "https://www.mitsubishielectric.com/psirt/vulnerability/pdf/2026-006_en.pdf".

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator owners, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately patch the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Industry observers note that mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately patch the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Vendor fixMitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions whitepaper on security vulnerabilities, which can be found at "https://iconics.com/about/security/cert".

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator customers, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

According to recent reports, mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately revision the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately patch the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

In a recent development, mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend configuring the PCs with the affected product installed so that only an administrator can log in, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately patch the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking remote login from untrusted networks and hosts, and from non-administrator owners, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. and allowing remote login only to administrator when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately patch the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend restricting physical access to the PC with the affected product installed and the network to which the PC is connected, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend preventing the user from clicking on web links in emails from untrusted sources, or from opening attachments in untrusted emails, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately upgrade the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend using the PCs with the affected product installed in the LAN and blocking access from untrusted networks and hosts through firewalls, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Sources indicate that mitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately enhancement the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend blocking unauthorized access by using a firewall, virtual private network (VPN), etc. when internet access is required, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

MitigationFor customers of products that do not have a fixed version or who cannot immediately update the product, Mitsubishi Electric and Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions recommend installing anti-virus software on the PC with the affected product installed, as well as on any PCs that can access it, to minimize the risk of exploitation of this vulnerability.

Product Status: known_affected.

Industry observers note that vendor fixMitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions is releasing fixed version 10.98 or later for GENESIS64, ICONICS Suite, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI. Please download the fixed version from the link below and install it: "https://iconicsinc.my.site.com/community/s/resource-center/product-downloads". (Note: GENESIS64, Hyper Historian, AnalytiX, and MobileHMI are included in the ICONICS Suite installation.) For more information on the fixed version, please refer to the Mitsubishi Electric Iconics Digital Solutions whitepaper on security vulnerabilities, which can be found at "https://iconics.com/about/security/cert".

Relevant CWE: CWE-266 Incorrect Privilege Assignment.

View CVE Details.

CISA recommends customers take defensive measures to minimize the risk of exploitation of these vulnerabilities.

CISA reminds organizations to perform proper impact analysis and risk assessment prior to deploying defensive measures.

CISA also provides a section for control systems security recommended practices on the ICS webpage on cisa.gov/ics. Several CISA products detailing cyber defense best practices are available for reading and download, including Improving Industrial Control Systems Cybersecurity with Defense-in-Depth Strategies.

CISA encourages organizations to implement recommended cybersecurity strategies for proactive defense of ICS assets.

Additional mitigation guidance and recommended practices are publicly available on the ICS webpage at cisa.gov/ics in the technical information paper, ICS-TIP-12-146-01B--Targeted Cyber Intrusion Detection and Mitigation Strategies.

Organizations observing suspected malicious activity should follow established internal procedures and report findings to CISA for tracking and correlation against other incidents.
